Abstract: Contact-implicit trajectory optimization (CITO) enables the automatic discovery of contact sequences, but most methods rely on fine time discretization to capture all contact events accurately, which increases problem size and runtime while tying solution quality to grid resolution. We extend the recently proposed sequential convex programming (SCP) approach for trajectory optimization, continuous-time successive convexification (ct-SCvx), to CITO by introducing integral cross-complementarity constraints, which eliminate the risk of missing contact events between discretization nodes while preserving the flexibility of contact mode changes. The resulting framework, contact-implicit successive convexification (ci-SCvx), models full multibody dynamics in maximal coordinates, including stick-slip friction and partially elastic impacts. To handle complementarity constraints, we embed a backtracking homotopy scheme within SCP for reliable convergence. We implement this framework in a stand-alone Python software, leveraging JAX for GPU acceleration and a custom canonical-form parser for the convex subproblems of SCP to avoid the overhead of general-purpose modeling tools such as CVXPY. We demonstrate ci-SCvx on diverse legged-locomotion tasks. In particular, we validate the approach in MuJoCo with the Gymnasium HalfCheetah model against the MuJoCo MPC baseline, showing that a tracking simulation with the optimized torque profiles from ci-SCvx produces physically consistent trajectories with lesser energy consumption. We also show that the resulting software achieves faster solve times than existing state-of-the-art SCP implementations by over an order of magnitude, thereby demonstrating a practically important contribution to scalable real-time trajectory optimization.

</details>


### [77] [Vision-Language-Action Model, Robustness, Multi-modal Learning, Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10055)
*Yuhan Xie,Yuping Yan,Yunqi Zhao,Handing Wang,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STRONG-VLA是一个解耦微调框架，通过分离鲁棒性获取与任务对齐优化，提升VLA模型在多模态扰动下的任务执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在多模态扰动（视觉损坏和语言噪声）下表现脆弱，现有鲁棒性方法通常依赖扰动数据联合训练，将鲁棒性视为静态目标，导致鲁棒性与任务保真度之间的优化冲突。

Method: 提出两阶段解耦微调框架：第一阶段通过难度递增的多模态扰动课程学习，在受控分布偏移下实现渐进鲁棒性学习；第二阶段与干净任务分布重新对齐，恢复执行保真度同时保持鲁棒性。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，STRONG-VLA显著提升多个VLA架构的任务成功率：OpenVLA在已见扰动下提升12.60%，未见扰动下提升7.77%；OpenVLA-OFT提升14.48%/13.81%；pi0提升16.49%/5.58%。真实世界机器人实验进一步验证其有效性。

Conclusion: 解耦优化对多模态鲁棒性至关重要，STRONG-VLA作为一个简单而原则性的框架，能够有效提升具身控制任务的鲁棒性，并展现出良好的跨架构泛化能力。

Abstract: Despite their strong performance in embodied tasks, recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remain highly fragile under multimodal perturbations, where visual corruption and linguistic noise jointly induce distribution shifts that degrade task-level execution. Existing robustness approaches typically rely on joint training with perturbed data, treating robustness as a static objective, which leads to conflicting optimization between robustness and task fidelity. In this work, we propose STRONG-VLA, a decoupled fine-tuning framework that explicitly separates robustness acquisition from task-aligned refinement. In Stage I, the model is exposed to a curriculum of multimodal perturbations with increasing difficulty, enabling progressive robustness learning under controlled distribution shifts. In Stage II, the model is re-aligned with clean task distributions to recover execution fidelity while preserving robustness. We further establish a comprehensive benchmark with 28 perturbation types spanning both textual and visual modalities, grounded in realistic sources of sensor noise, occlusion, and instruction corruption. Extensive experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that STRONG-VLA consistently improves task success rates across multiple VLA architectures. On OpenVLA, our method achieves gains of up to 12.60% under seen perturbations and 7.77% under unseen perturbations. Notably, similar or larger improvements are observed on OpenVLA-OFT (+14.48% / +13.81%) and pi0 (+16.49% / +5.58%), demonstrating strong cross-architecture generalization. Real-world experiments on an AIRBOT robotic platform further validate its practical effectiveness. These results highlight the importance of decoupled optimization for multimodal robustness and establish STRONG-VLA as a simple yet principled framework for robust embodied control.

</details>


### [78] [Natural Gradient Gaussian Approximation Filter on Lie Groups for Robot State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10057)
*Tianyi Zhang,Wenhan Cao,Chang Liu,Yao Lyu,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NANO-L滤波器，通过将流形滤波重构为高斯分布增量变量的参数优化问题，避免线性化，利用自然梯度优化和Fisher信息矩阵处理切空间曲率，在不变观测模型下获得闭式协方差更新，显著降低估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Lie群流形上演化的机器人系统（如腿式机器人）需要精确状态估计以实现敏捷控制。现有滤波器依赖切空间局部线性化处理流形上的非线性观测模型，导致累积估计误差。

Method: 将流形滤波重构为高斯分布增量变量的参数优化问题，避免线性化。增量通过指数映射到Lie群，以乘法方式作用于先验估计得到后验状态。提出自然梯度优化方案，利用增量变量的Fisher信息矩阵考虑切空间曲率，得到NANO-L滤波器。对于机器人定位任务中广泛采用的不变观测模型，利用Lie导数扰动模型证明协方差更新存在精确闭式解。

Result: 在Unitree GO2腿式机器人不同地形上的硬件实验表明，NANO-L在可比计算成本下，相比常用滤波器实现约40%的估计误差降低。

Conclusion: NANO-L滤波器通过避免线性化和利用自然梯度优化，有效处理Lie群流形上的非线性观测模型，在不变观测模型下获得闭式协方差更新，显著提高状态估计精度，适用于腿式机器人等需要在流形上演化的系统。

Abstract: Accurate state estimation for robotic systems evolving on Lie group manifolds, such as legged robots, is a prerequisite for achieving agile control. However, this task is challenged by nonlinear observation models defined on curved manifolds, where existing filters rely on local linearization in the tangent space to handle such nonlinearity, leading to accumulated estimation errors. To address this limitation, we reformulate manifold filtering as a parameter optimization problem over a Gaussian-distributed increment variable, thereby avoiding linearization. Under this formulation, the increment can be mapped to the Lie group through the exponential operator, where it acts multiplicatively on the prior estimate to yield the posterior state. We further propose a natural gradient optimization scheme for solving this problem, whose iteration process leverages the Fisher information matrix of the increment variable to account for the curvature of the tangent space. This results in an iterative algorithm named the Natural Gradient Gaussian Approximation on Lie Groups (NANO-L) filter. Leveraging the perturbation model in Lie derivative, we prove that for the invariant observation model widely adopted in robotic localization tasks, the covariance update in NANO-L admits an exact closed-form solution, eliminating the need for iterative updates thus improving computational efficiency. Hardware experiments on a Unitree GO2 legged robot operating across different terrains demonstrate that NANO-L achieves approximately 40% lower estimation error than commonly used filters at a comparable computational cost.

</details>


### [79] [A Ray Intersection Algorithm for Fast Growth Distance Computation Between Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10058)
*Akshay Thirugnanam,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种计算凸集间增长距离的高效算法，通过将问题转化为闵可夫斯基差集上的射线相交问题，使用内外多面体逼近迭代求解，在机器人运动规划和刚体仿真中应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增长距离作为凸集相交与分离的统一度量，比最小距离提供更全面的信息。现有方法计算效率有限，需要开发高效算法支持机器人等实际应用。

Method: 将增长距离问题转化为闵可夫斯基差集上的射线相交问题，通过迭代构造闵可夫斯基差集的内外多面体逼近来求解，算法保持原始和对偶可行性及单调收敛性。

Result: 算法在广泛凸集基准测试中达到最先进性能，开源实现高效可靠，成功应用于机器人运动规划和刚体仿真任务。

Conclusion: 提出的增长距离算法高效且理论性质良好，为凸集相交分析提供了统一框架，在机器人等领域具有实用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we discuss an efficient algorithm for computing the growth distance between two compact convex sets with representable support functions. The growth distance between two sets is the minimum scaling factor such that the sets intersect when scaled about some center points. Unlike the minimum distance between sets, the growth distance provides a unified measure for set intersection and separation. We first reduce the growth distance problem to an equivalent ray intersection problem on the Minkowski difference set. Then, we propose an algorithm to solve the ray intersection problem by iteratively constructing inner and outer polyhedral approximations of the Minkowski difference set. We show that our algorithm satisfies several key properties, such as primal and dual feasibility and monotone convergence. We provide extensive benchmark results for our algorithm and show that our open-source implementation achieves state-of-the-art performance across a wide variety of convex sets. Finally, we demonstrate robotics applications of our algorithm in motion planning and rigid-body simulation.

</details>


### [80] [MoRI: Mixture of RL and IL Experts for Long-Horizon Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10165)
*Yaohang Xu,Lianjie Ma,Gewei Zuo,Wentao Zhang,Han Ding,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoRI是一种结合强化学习和模仿学习的混合专家系统，通过动态切换IL和RL专家来处理机器人操作任务中的粗粒度运动和细粒度操作，显著减少人类干预并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习(IL)存在复合误差和分布偏移问题，而强化学习(RL)样本效率低且试错成本高。现有混合方法在处理复杂任务时表现不佳，需要一种能结合两者优势的新方法。

Method: 提出MoRI系统：1) 基于专家动作方差动态切换IL和RL专家；2) 采用离线预训练和在线微调两阶段训练；3) 对RL组件应用IL正则化以保证探索安全性和减少人工干预。

Result: 在四个复杂真实世界任务中，MoRI平均成功率97.5%，仅需2-5小时微调。相比基线RL算法，减少85.8%人工干预，缩短21%收敛时间。

Conclusion: MoRI通过动态混合IL和RL专家，有效解决了机器人操作中IL的分布偏移问题和RL的低效探索问题，在复杂任务中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) and Imitation Learning (IL) are the standard frameworks for policy acquisition in manipulation. While IL offers efficient policy derivation, it suffers from compounding errors and distribution shift. Conversely, RL facilitates autonomous exploration but is frequently hindered by low sample efficiency and the high cost of trial and error. Since existing hybrid methods often struggle with complex tasks, we introduce Mixture of RL and IL Experts (MoRI). This system dynamically switches between IL and RL experts based on the variance of expert actions to handle coarse movements and fine-grained manipulations. MoRI employs an offline pre-training stage followed by online fine-tuning to accelerate convergence. To maintain exploration safety and minimize human intervention, the system applies IL-based regularization to the RL component. Evaluation across four complex real-world tasks shows that MoRI achieves an average success rate of 97.5% within 2 to 5 hours of fine-tuning. Compared to baseline RL algorithms, MoRI reduces human intervention by 85.8% and shortens convergence time by 21%, demonstrating its capability in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [81] [Device-Conditioned Neural Architecture Search for Efficient Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10170)
*Yiming Wu,Huan Wang,Zhenghao Chen,Ge Yuan,Dong Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DC-QFA：一种设备感知的量化框架，通过单一超网支持异构硬件部署，无需设备特定优化，实现2-3倍加速且性能损失可忽略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作模型部署方法存在设备特定、泛化性差、需要耗时设备优化等问题，需要统一的跨硬件部署框架

Method: 提出设备感知量化训练和硬件约束架构搜索的统一框架，构建单一超网覆盖架构和混合精度设计空间，使用延迟和内存感知正则化，通过多步策略蒸馏减少低精度下的误差累积

Result: 在三种代表性策略骨干上实现2-3倍加速（边缘设备、消费级GPU、云平台），任务成功率损失可忽略；真实机器人实验验证低比特策略在接触丰富操作中的稳定性

Conclusion: DC-QFA为异构硬件提供高效、通用的机器人策略部署方案，显著减少部署时间，保持性能稳定

Abstract: The growing complexity of visuomotor policies poses significant challenges for deployment with heterogeneous robotic hardware constraints. However, most existing model-efficient approaches for robotic manipulation are device- and model-specific, lack generalizability, and require time-consuming per-device optimization during the adaptation process. In this work, we propose a unified framework named \textbf{D}evice-\textbf{C}onditioned \textbf{Q}uantization-\textbf{F}or-\textbf{A}ll (DC-QFA) which amortizes deployment effort with the device-conditioned quantization-aware training and hardware-constrained architecture search. Specifically, we introduce a single supernet that spans a rich design space over network architectures and mixed-precision bit-widths. It is optimized with latency- and memory-aware regularization, guided by per-device lookup tables. With this supernet, for each target platform, we can perform a once-for-all lightweight search to select an optimal subnet without any per-device re-optimization, which enables more generalizable deployment across heterogeneous hardware, and substantially reduces deployment time. To improve long-horizon stability under low precision, we further introduce multi-step on-policy distillation to mitigate error accumulation during closed-loop execution. Extensive experiments on three representative policy backbones, such as DiffusionPolicy-T, MDT-V, and OpenVLA-OFT, demonstrate that our DC-QFA achieves $2\text{-}3\times$ acceleration on edge devices, consumer-grade GPUs, and cloud platforms, with negligible performance drop in task success. Real-world evaluations on an Inovo robot equipped with a force/torque sensor further validates that our low-bit DC-QFA policies maintain stable, contact-rich manipulation even under severe quantization.

</details>


### [82] [ReaLiTy and LADS: A Unified Framework and Dataset Suite for LiDAR Adaptation Across Sensors and Adverse Weather Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10213)
*Vivek Anand,Bharat Lohani,Rakesh Mishra,Gaurav Pandey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReaLiTy是一个统一的物理信息框架，用于将LiDAR数据转换为匹配目标传感器规格和天气条件，并附带LiDAR适应数据集套件(LADS)用于系统研究领域偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集很少提供相同场景在不同传感器配置和天气条件下的物理一致观测，限制了领域偏移的系统分析，而可靠的LiDAR感知需要在传感器、环境和恶劣天气条件下具有鲁棒性。

Method: 提出ReaLiTy框架，整合物理基础线索与学习模块生成真实强度模式，同时使用基于物理的天气模型引入一致的几何和辐射度退化；基于此框架创建LiDAR适应数据集套件(LADS)，提供物理一致、可转换的点云数据。

Result: 实验证明该方法提高了跨域一致性并生成了真实的天气效果，ReaLiTy和LADS为研究LiDAR适应和智能交通系统中的仿真驱动感知提供了可复现的基础。

Conclusion: 该工作通过物理信息框架和配套数据集解决了LiDAR感知中的领域偏移问题，为系统研究传感器配置和天气条件变化对感知性能的影响提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Reliable LiDAR perception requires robustness across sensors, environments, and adverse weather. However, existing datasets rarely provide physically consistent observations of the same scene under varying sensor configurations and weather conditions, limiting systematic analysis of domain shifts. This work presents ReaLiTy, a unified physics-informed framework that transforms LiDAR data to match target sensor specifications and weather conditions. The framework integrates physically grounded cues with a learning-based module to generate realistic intensity patterns, while a physics-based weather model introduces consistent geometric and radiometric degradations. Building on this framework, we introduce the LiDAR Adaptation Dataset Suite (LADS), a collection of physically consistent, transformation-ready point clouds with one-to-one correspondence to original datasets. Experiments demonstrate improved cross-domain consistency and realistic weather effects. ReaLiTy and LADS provide a reproducible foundation for studying LiDAR adaptation and simulation-driven perception in intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [83] [A Coordinate-Invariant Local Representation of Motion and Force Trajectories for Identification and Generalization Across Coordinate Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10241)
*Arno Verduyn,Erwin Aertbeliën,Maxim Vochten,Joris De Schutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新的双上三角不变表示(DUTIR)，用于刚性体和相互作用力轨迹的坐标不变表示，具有更好的奇异性鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人学、生物力学等领域中，识别刚性体和相互作用力的轨迹对于轨迹分割、识别和预测等任务至关重要。这些任务面临的关键挑战是：当轨迹在不同坐标系中表达时，如何获得一致的结果。坐标不变表示可以解决这一问题，但现有方法通常对测量噪声敏感，且在表示中存在奇异性问题。

Method: 提出新颖的双上三角不变表示(DUTIR)，这是一种坐标不变表示方法，具有改进的奇异性鲁棒性。该方法将轨迹转换为坐标不变表示，并在抽象层面上进行公式化，使其既适用于刚性体轨迹，也适用于相互作用力轨迹。论文还提供了相应的计算算法。

Result: DUTIR表示方法能够为刚性体轨迹和相互作用力轨迹提供坐标不变表示，相比现有方法具有更好的奇异性鲁棒性，减少了噪声敏感性，避免了表示不唯一的问题。

Conclusion: DUTIR是一种通用的坐标不变表示工具，适用于机器人学、生物力学及相关领域，能够有效解决轨迹分析中的坐标系依赖性和奇异性问题，为轨迹分割、识别和预测等任务提供了更稳健的基础。

Abstract: Identifying the trajectories of rigid bodies and of interaction forces is essential for a wide range of tasks in robotics, biomechanics, and related domains. These tasks include trajectory segmentation, recognition, and prediction. For these tasks, a key challenge lies in achieving consistent results when the trajectory is expressed in different coordinate systems. A way to address this challenge is to utilize trajectory models that can generalize across coordinate systems. The focus of this paper is on such trajectory models obtained by transforming the trajectory into a coordinate-invariant representation. However, coordinate-invariant representations often suffer from sensitivity to measurement noise and the manifestation of singularities in the representation, where the representation is not uniquely defined. This paper aims to address this limitation by introducing the novel Dual-Upper-Triangular Invariant Representation (DUTIR), with improved robustness to singularities, along with its computational algorithm. The proposed representation is formulated at a level of abstraction that makes it applicable to both rigid-body trajectories and interaction-force trajectories, hence making it a versatile tool for robotics, biomechanics, and related domains.

</details>


### [84] [Trajectory-based actuator identification via differentiable simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10351)
*Vyacheslav Kovalev,Ekaterina Chaikovskaia,Egor Davydenko,Roman Gorbachev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于可微分仿真的轨迹匹配方法，仅使用编码器运动数据即可识别系统级执行器模型，无需扭矩传感器或电流电压测量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真执行器模型对缩小仿真与真实机器人行为差距至关重要，但传统方法需要专用测试台和扭矩传感设备，成本高且复杂

Method: 将识别问题转化为轨迹匹配问题：给定指令关节位置和测量的关节角度/速度，通过可微分仿真反向传播优化执行器和仿真器参数；支持从紧凑结构化参数化到神经执行器映射的多种模型类别

Result: 在相同指令下的真实机器人轨迹测试中，无扭矩传感器识别方法将平均绝对位置误差从14.20 mrad降低至7.54 mrad（提升1.88倍）；在真实机器人运动研究中，使用精炼执行器模型训练策略使行进距离增加46%，旋转偏差减少75%

Conclusion: 基于可微分仿真的轨迹匹配方法能够仅从编码器数据中有效识别执行器模型，显著提升仿真与真实机器人行为的一致性，为机器人控制策略训练提供更准确的仿真环境

Abstract: Accurate actuation models are critical for bridging the gap between simulation and real robot behavior, yet obtaining high-fidelity actuator dynamics typically requires dedicated test stands and torque sensing. We present a trajectory-based actuator identification method that uses differentiable simulation to fit system-level actuator models from encoder motion alone. Identification is posed as a trajectory-matching problem: given commanded joint positions and measured joint angles and velocities, we optimize actuator and simulator parameters by backpropagating through the simulator, without torque sensors, current/voltage measurements, or access to embedded motor-control internals. The framework supports multiple model classes, ranging from compact structured parameterizations to neural actuator mappings, within a unified optimization pipeline. On held-out real-robot trajectories under identical commands, the proposed torque-sensor-free identification achieves much tighter trajectory alignment than a supervised stand-trained baseline dominated by steady-state data, reducing mean absolute position error from 14.20 mrad to as low as 7.54 mrad (1.88 times). Finally, we demonstrate downstream impact in a real-robot locomotion study: training policies with the refined actuator model increases travel distance by 46% and reduces rotational deviation by 75% relative to the baseline.

</details>


### [85] [AnySlot: Goal-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Policies for Zero-Shot Slot-Level Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10432)
*Zhaofeng Hu,Sifan Zhou,Qinbo Zhang,Rongtao Xu,Qi Su,Ci-Jyun Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnySlot框架通过引入显式空间视觉目标作为中间表示，将语言指令转换为场景标记，再用目标条件VLA策略执行，显著提升了组合式语言指令下的精确物体放置能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前单一VLA策略在组合式语言指令下的精确物体放置（槽位级任务）面临挑战，需要可靠的槽位定位和亚厘米级执行精度。现有方法在处理这类需要结构化空间推理的任务时表现不佳。

Method: 提出AnySlot框架：1）将语言转换为显式视觉目标（生成场景标记）；2）使用目标条件VLA策略执行该目标。这种分层设计将高级槽位选择与低级执行解耦。同时创建SlotBench基准，包含9个任务类别来评估结构化空间推理。

Result: 大量实验表明，AnySlot在零样本槽位级放置任务中显著优于扁平VLA基线和先前的模块化定位方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入显式空间视觉目标作为中间表示，AnySlot有效解决了组合式语言指令下的精确物体放置问题，其分层设计确保了语义准确性和空间鲁棒性，为槽位级任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have emerged as a versatile paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation. However, precise object placement under compositional language instructions remains a major challenge for modern monolithic VLA policies. Slot-level tasks require both reliable slot grounding and sub-centimeter execution accuracy. To this end, we propose AnySlot, a framework that reduces compositional complexity by introducing an explicit spatial visual goal as an intermediate representation between language grounding and control. AnySlot turns language into an explicit visual goal by generating a scene marker, then executes this goal with a goal-conditioned VLA policy. This hierarchical design effectively decouples high-level slot selection from low-level execution, ensuring both semantic accuracy and spatial robustness. Furthermore, recognizing the lack of existing benchmarks for such precision-demanding tasks, we introduce SlotBench, a comprehensive simulation benchmark featuring nine task categories tailored to evaluate structured spatial reasoning in slot-level placement. Extensive experiments show that AnySlot significantly outperforms flat VLA baselines and previous modular grounding methods in zero-shot slot-level placement.

</details>


### [86] [PRoID: Predicted Rate of Information Delivery in Multi-Robot Exploration and Relaying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10433)
*Seungchan Kim,Seungjae Baek,Micah Corah,Graeme Best,Brady Moon,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PRoID和PRoID-Safe算法解决多机器人探索与中继问题，通过预测信息增益和生存概率优化中继时机决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多机器人探索与中继（MRER）问题中的核心挑战：决定机器人何时停止探索并开始中继信息。现有方法要么忽略报告要求，要么依赖无法适应环境结构、团队组成或任务进度的固定调度策略。

Method: 提出PRoID（预测信息交付率）中继准则，使用学习的地图预测来估计每个机器人沿规划路径的未来信息增益，并考虑队友正在中继的信息。PRoID在即时返回能产生更高单位时间信息交付时触发中继。进一步提出PRoID-Safe，将机器人生存概率纳入中继准则，在故障风险增加时自然偏向更早中继。

Result: 在真实室内平面图数据集上评估，PRoID和PRoID-Safe优于固定调度基线方法，在故障场景中表现出更强的相对优势。

Conclusion: PRoID和PRoID-Safe通过预测信息增益和考虑故障风险，有效解决了多机器人探索与中继问题中的自适应中继决策挑战，在真实场景中显著优于传统固定调度方法。

Abstract: We address Multi-Robot Exploration and Relaying (MRER): a team of robots must explore an unknown environment and deliver acquired information to a fixed base station within a mission time limit. The central challenge is deciding when each robot should stop exploring and relay: this depends on what the robot is likely to find ahead, what information it uniquely holds, and whether immediate or future delivery is more valuable. Prior approaches either ignore the reporting requirement entirely or rely on fixed-schedule relay strategies that cannot adapt to environment structure, team composition, or mission progress. We introduce PRoID (Predicted Rate of Information Delivery), a relay criterion that uses learned map prediction to estimate each robot's future information gain along its planned path, accounting for what teammates are already relaying. PRoID triggers relay when immediate return yields higher information delivery per unit time. We further propose PRoID-Safe, a failure-aware extension that incorporates robot survival probability into the relay criterion, naturally biasing decisions toward earlier relay as failure risk grows. We evaluate on real-world indoor floor plan datasets and show that PRoID and PRoID-Safe outperform fixed-schedule baselines, with stronger relative gains in failure scenarios.

</details>


### [87] [VLN-NF: Feasibility-Aware Vision-and-Language Navigation with False-Premise Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10533)
*Hung-Ting Su,Ting-Jun Wang,Jia-Fong Yeh,Min Sun,Winston H. Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLN-NF基准测试，针对视觉语言导航中的错误前提指令问题，要求智能体在目标不存在时进行探索并输出NOT-FOUND，同时提出ROAM方法解决该挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉语言导航基准假设指令可行且目标存在，导致智能体无法处理错误前提目标。需要新基准来评估智能体在目标不存在时的导航、探索和决策能力。

Method: 1) 构建VLN-NF基准：使用LLM重写VLN指令，VLM验证目标不存在，生成合理但事实错误的目标指令；2) 提出REV-SPL评估指标：联合评估房间到达、探索覆盖和决策正确性；3) 提出ROAM方法：两阶段混合方法，结合监督式房间级导航和LLM/VLM驱动的室内探索，利用自由空间清除先验引导。

Result: ROAM方法在REV-SPL指标上表现最佳，而基线方法通常在不可靠指令下探索不足且提前终止。VLN-NF基准有效评估了智能体处理错误前提指令的能力。

Conclusion: VLN-NF基准填补了视觉语言导航中处理错误前提指令的空白，ROAM方法通过结合监督导航和LLM/VLM驱动的探索，有效解决了目标不存在时的导航挑战。

Abstract: Conventional Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) benchmarks assume instructions are feasible and the referenced target exists, leaving agents ill-equipped to handle false-premise goals. We introduce VLN-NF, a benchmark with false-premise instructions where the target is absent from the specified room and agents must navigate, gather evidence through in-room exploration, and explicitly output NOT-FOUND. VLN-NF is constructed via a scalable pipeline that rewrites VLN instructions using an LLM and verifies target absence with a VLM, producing plausible yet factually incorrect goals. We further propose REV-SPL to jointly evaluate room reaching, exploration coverage, and decision correctness. To address this challenge, we present ROAM, a two-stage hybrid that combines supervised room-level navigation with LLM/VLM-driven in-room exploration guided by a free-space clearance prior. ROAM achieves the best REV-SPL among compared methods, while baselines often under-explore and terminate prematurely under unreliable instructions. VLN-NF project page can be found at https://vln-nf.github.io/.

</details>


### [88] [AffordGen: Generating Diverse Demonstrations for Generalizable Object Manipulation with Afford Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10579)
*Jiawei Zhang,Kaizhe Hu,Yingqian Huang,Yuanchen Ju,Zhengrong Xue,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordGen框架利用3D生成模型和视觉基础模型，通过大规模3D网格中有意义关键点的语义对应关系生成新的机器人操作轨迹，解决模仿学习中因数据多样性不足导致的几何变化限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代模仿学习方法在机器人操作中取得了成功，但其性能常因数据多样性有限而受到几何变化的限制。需要一种能够生成多样化操作数据的方法来提升策略的泛化能力。

Method: 提出AffordGen框架：1) 利用3D生成模型和视觉基础模型(VFMs)，通过大规模3D网格中有意义关键点的语义对应关系生成新的机器人操作轨迹；2) 构建大规模、具备可供性感知的数据集；3) 训练鲁棒的闭环视觉运动策略，结合可供性的语义泛化能力和端到端学习的反应鲁棒性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的实验中，使用AffordGen训练的策略实现了高成功率，并能够零样本泛化到完全未见过的物体，显著提高了机器人学习的数据效率。

Conclusion: AffordGen通过结合3D生成模型、视觉基础模型和可供性概念，有效解决了模仿学习中的数据多样性限制问题，实现了对未见物体的零样本泛化，为机器人学习提供了高效的数据生成方法。

Abstract: Despite the recent success of modern imitation learning methods in robot manipulation, their performance is often constrained by geometric variations due to limited data diversity. Leveraging powerful 3D generative models and vision foundation models (VFMs), the proposed AffordGen framework overcomes this limitation by utilizing the semantic correspondence of meaningful keypoints across large-scale 3D meshes to generate new robot manipulation trajectories. This large-scale, affordance-aware dataset is then used to train a robust, closed-loop visuomotor policy, combining the semantic generalizability of affordances with the reactive robustness of end-to-end learning. Experiments in simulation and the real world show that policies trained with AffordGen achieve high success rates and enable zero-shot generalization to truly unseen objects, significantly improving data efficiency in robot learning.

</details>


### [89] [MonoEM-GS: Monocular Expectation-Maximization Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10593)
*Evgenii Kruzhkov,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoEM-GS：一种单目建图管道，将前馈几何基础模型的预测集成到全局高斯泼溅表示中，通过EM公式稳定几何，使用ICP进行位姿估计，并支持多模态特征查询


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈几何基础模型可以从RGB流中直接推断密集点云和相机运动，为单目SLAM提供先验。但这些预测存在视图依赖性和噪声问题：几何在不同视角和图像变换下会变化，局部度量属性在帧间可能漂移。

Method: MonoEM-GS将高斯泼溅与期望最大化（EM）公式耦合以稳定几何，采用基于ICP的对齐方法进行单目位姿估计。高斯泼溅参数化包含多模态特征，支持在重建地图上直接进行开放集分割和其他下游查询。

Result: 在7-Scenes、TUM RGB-D和Replica数据集上评估MonoEM-GS，并与近期基线方法进行比较。

Conclusion: MonoEM-GS成功解决了前馈几何基础模型预测的视图依赖性和噪声问题，通过EM稳定几何和ICP对齐实现了鲁棒的单目建图，同时支持多模态特征查询功能。

Abstract: Feed-forward geometric foundation models can infer dense point clouds and camera motion directly from RGB streams, providing priors for monocular SLAM. However, their predictions are often view-dependent and noisy: geometry can vary across viewpoints and under image transformations, and local metric properties may drift between frames. We present MonoEM-GS, a monocular mapping pipeline that integrates such geometric predictions into a global Gaussian Splatting representation while explicitly addressing these inconsistencies. MonoEM-GS couples Gaussian Splatting with an Expectation--Maximization formulation to stabilize geometry, and employs ICP-based alignment for monocular pose estimation. Beyond geometry, MonoEM-GS parameterizes Gaussians with multi-modal features, enabling in-place open-set segmentation and other downstream queries directly on the reconstructed map. We evaluate MonoEM-GS on 7-Scenes, TUM RGB-D and Replica, and compare against recent baselines.

</details>


### [90] [OmniUMI: Towards Physically Grounded Robot Learning via Human-Aligned Multimodal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10647)
*Shaqi Luo,Yuanyuan Li,Youhao Hu,Chenhao Yu,Chaoran Xu,Jiachen Zhang,Guocai Yao,Tiejun Huang,Ran He,Zhongyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniUMI：通过人类对齐的多模态交互实现物理基础机器人学习的统一框架，同步采集RGB、深度、轨迹、触觉、内部抓取力和外部交互力矩，支持接触丰富的操作任务学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UMI风格界面主要依赖视觉和轨迹，对物理交互信号访问有限，这在接触丰富的操作任务中成为根本限制，因为成功依赖于难以仅从视觉推断的接触动力学（触觉交互、内部抓取力、外部交互力矩）。

Method: 开发紧凑手持系统同步采集RGB、深度、轨迹、触觉感知、内部抓取力和外部交互力矩；通过共享体现设计保持采集-部署一致性；提供双力反馈（双边夹持器反馈和手持体现中的外部交互力矩自然感知）；基于扩散策略扩展视觉、触觉和力相关观测；通过基于阻抗的执行部署学习策略以统一调节运动和接触行为。

Result: 实验展示了可靠的传感能力和在力敏感拾放、交互式表面擦除和触觉信息选择性释放任务上的强大下游性能。

Conclusion: OmniUMI将物理基础的多模态数据采集与人类对齐交互相结合，为学习接触丰富的操作提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: UMI-style interfaces enable scalable robot learning, but existing systems remain largely visuomotor, relying primarily on RGB observations and trajectory while providing only limited access to physical interaction signals. This becomes a fundamental limitation in contact-rich manipulation, where success depends on contact dynamics such as tactile interaction, internal grasping force, and external interaction wrench that are difficult to infer from vision alone. We present OmniUMI, a unified framework for physically grounded robot learning via human-aligned multimodal interaction. OmniUMI synchronously captures RGB, depth, trajectory, tactile sensing, internal grasping force, and external interaction wrench within a compact handheld system, while maintaining collection--deployment consistency through a shared embodiment design. To support human-aligned demonstration, OmniUMI provides dual-force feedback through bilateral gripper feedback and natural perception of external interaction wrench in the handheld embodiment. Built on this interface, we extend diffusion policy with visual, tactile, and force-related observations, and deploy the learned policy through impedance-based execution for unified regulation of motion and contact behavior. Experiments demonstrate reliable sensing and strong downstream performance on force-sensitive pick-and-place, interactive surface erasing, and tactile-informed selective release. Overall, OmniUMI combines physically grounded multimodal data acquisition with human-aligned interaction, providing a scalable foundation for learning contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [91] [LIDEA: Human-to-Robot Imitation Learning via Implicit Feature Distillation and Explicit Geometry Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10677)
*Yifu Xu,Bokai Lin,Xinyu Zhan,Hongjie Fang,Yong-Lu Li,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIDEA框架通过隐式特征蒸馏和显式几何对齐，利用人类视频数据替代机器人演示，解决跨具身模仿学习中的视觉和几何差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习面临演示数据稀缺问题，而人类视频数据丰富但存在具身差异。现有跨具身迁移方法依赖视觉编辑，但会因视觉外观和3D几何差异产生伪影。

Method: 提出LIDEA框架：1) 2D视觉域采用双阶段传递蒸馏管道，在共享潜在空间对齐人类和机器人表示；2) 3D几何域提出具身无关对齐策略，将具身与交互几何解耦，确保一致的3D感知。

Result: 实验验证：人类数据可替代高达80%的昂贵机器人演示；框架成功从人类视频迁移未见模式，实现分布外泛化。

Conclusion: LIDEA通过隐式特征蒸馏和显式几何对齐有效解决跨具身模仿学习问题，显著减少对机器人演示的依赖，提升数据效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Scaling up robot learning is hindered by the scarcity of robotic demonstrations, whereas human videos offer a vast, untapped source of interaction data. However, bridging the embodiment gap between human hands and robot arms remains a critical challenge. Existing cross-embodiment transfer strategies typically rely on visual editing, but they often introduce visual artifacts due to intrinsic discrepancies in visual appearance and 3D geometry. To address these limitations, we introduce LIDEA (Implicit Feature Distillation and Explicit Geometric Alignment), an imitation learning framework in which policy learning benefits from human demonstrations. In the 2D visual domain, LIDEA employs a dual-stage transitive distillation pipeline that aligns human and robot representations in a shared latent space. In the 3D geometric domain, we propose an embodiment-agnostic alignment strategy that explicitly decouples embodiment from interaction geometry, ensuring consistent 3D-aware perception. Extensive experiments empirically validate LIDEA from two perspectives: data efficiency and OOD robustness. Results show that human data substitutes up to 80% of costly robot demonstrations, and the framework successfully transfers unseen patterns from human videos for out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [92] [WARPED: Wrist-Aligned Rendering for Robot Policy Learning from Egocentric Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10809)
*Harry Freeman,Chung Hee Kim,George Kantor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WARPED框架利用单目RGB视频合成逼真的腕部视角观测，用于训练视觉运动策略，减少数据收集时间5-8倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从人类演示学习的方法通常依赖多视角相机、深度传感器或定制硬件，且局限于第三人称或自我中心相机视角，数据收集成本高且可扩展性有限

Method: 1) 使用自我中心RGB相机收集数据；2) 利用视觉基础模型初始化交互场景；3) 通过手-物体交互管道跟踪手和物体，并将轨迹重定向到机器人末端执行器；4) 通过高斯泼溅合成逼真的腕部视角观测来训练机器人策略

Result: 在五个桌面操作任务中，WARPED达到与遥操作演示数据训练策略相当的成功率，同时数据收集时间减少5-8倍

Conclusion: WARPED框架仅使用单目RGB数据就能合成逼真的腕部视角观测，有效降低了机器人策略训练的数据收集成本，为从人类演示学习提供了更高效的方法

Abstract: Recent advancements in learning from human demonstration have shown promising results in addressing the scalability and high cost of data collection required to train robust visuomotor policies. However, existing approaches are often constrained by a reliance on multiview camera setups, depth sensors, or custom hardware and are typically limited to policy execution from third-person or egocentric cameras. In this paper, we present WARPED, a framework designed to synthesize realistic wrist-view observations from human demonstration videos to facilitate the training of visuomotor policies using only monocular RGB data. With data collected from an egocentric RGB camera, our system leverages vision foundation models to initialize the interactive scene. A hand-object interaction pipeline is then employed to track the hand and manipulated object and retarget the trajectories to a robotic end-effector. Lastly, photo-realistic wrist-view observations are synthesized via Gaussian Splatting to directly train a robotic policy. We demonstrate that WARPED achieves success rates comparable to policies trained on teleoperated demonstration data for five tabletop manipulation tasks, while requiring 5-8x less data collection time.

</details>


### [93] [BridgeSim: Unveiling the OL-CL Gap in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10856)
*Seth Z. Zhao,Luobin Wang,Hongwei Ruan,Yuxin Bao,Yilan Chen,Ziyang Leng,Abhijit Ravichandran,Honglin He,Zewei Zhou,Xu Han,Abhishek Peri,Zhiyu Huang,Pranav Desai,Henrik Christensen,Jiaqi Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文揭示了开环到闭环差距的根本原因（观测域偏移和目标不匹配），并提出了一种测试时自适应框架来缓解规划偏差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开环预训练策略在开环评估中得分很高，但在闭环部署中却无法有效转移，这种系统性失败需要深入分析和解决

Method: 提出测试时自适应框架，通过校准观测偏移、减少状态-动作偏差和强制时间一致性来缓解规划偏差

Result: TTA有效缓解了规划偏差，展现出比基线方法更优越的扩展动态，并揭示了标准开环评估协议中的盲点

Conclusion: 开环策略存在结构性的目标不匹配问题，测试时自适应是解决开环-闭环差距的有效方法，需要重新审视开环评估协议

Abstract: Open-loop (OL) to closed-loop (CL) gap (OL-CL gap) exists when OL-pretrained policies scoring high in OL evaluations fail to transfer effectively in closed-loop (CL) deployment. In this paper, we unveil the root causes of this systemic failure and propose a practical remedy. Specifically, we demonstrate that OL policies suffer from Observational Domain Shift and Objective Mismatch. We show that while the former is largely recoverable with adaptation techniques, the latter creates a structural inability to model complex reactive behaviors, which forms the primary OL-CL gap. We find that a wide range of OL policies learn a biased Q-value estimator that neglects both the reactive nature of CL simulations and the temporal awareness needed to reduce compounding errors. To this end, we propose a Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) framework that calibrates observational shift, reduces state-action biases, and enforces temporal consistency. Extensive experiments show that TTA effectively mitigates planning biases and yields superior scaling dynamics than its baseline counterparts. Furthermore, our analysis highlights the existence of blind spots in standard OL evaluation protocols that fail to capture the realities of closed-loop deployment.

</details>


### [94] [HECTOR: Human-centric Hierarchical Coordination and Supervision of Robotic Fleets under Continual Temporal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10892)
*Shen Wang,Yinhang Luo,Jie Li,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HECTOR框架：用于大规模机器人舰队在持续不确定时序任务下的人为中心协调与监督方案，包含三层分层结构实现人机交互、任务分配和动态协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人舰队在并发协作时效率极高，但操作员直接控制每个机器人不现实。现有文献缺乏对操作员在线交互（添加/取消任务、修改优先级等）和高效算法设计的关注，特别是在动态部分未知环境中。

Method: 提出三层分层框架HECTOR：(I)双向多模态人机交互协议，操作员监督整个舰队；(II)滚动分配当前已知任务给团队；(III)在线执行中基于检测到的子任务进行团队内动态协调。支持时序逻辑公式描述的协作动作。

Result: 通过大量人在环仿真实验，在异构舰队、多种时序任务和环境不确定性条件下验证了框架有效性。分层结构允许不同粒度的人机交互和监督，提高计算效率并减少人工负担。

Conclusion: HECTOR框架成功解决了大规模机器人舰队在持续不确定时序任务下的人为中心协调与监督问题，实现了高效的人机交互、任务分配和动态协调，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robotic fleets can be extremely efficient when working concurrently and collaboratively, e.g., for delivery, surveillance, search and rescue. However, it can be demanding or even impractical for an operator to directly control each robot. Thus, autonomy of the fleet and its online interaction with the operator are both essential, particularly in dynamic and partially unknown environments. The operator might need to add new tasks, cancel some tasks, change priorities and modify planning results. How to design the procedure for these interactions and efficient algorithms to fulfill these needs have been mostly neglected in the related literature. Thus, this work proposes a human-centric coordination and supervision scheme (HECTOR) for large-scale robotic fleets under continual and uncertain temporal tasks. It consists of three hierarchical layers: (I) the bidirectional and multimodal protocol of online human-fleet interaction, where the operator interacts with and supervises the whole fleet; (II) the rolling assignment of currently-known tasks to teams within a certain horizon, and (III) the dynamic coordination within a team given the detected subtasks during online execution. The overall mission can be as general as temporal logic formulas over collaborative actions. Such hierarchical structure allows human interaction and supervision at different granularities and triggering conditions, to both improve computational efficiency and reduce human effort. Extensive human-in-the-loop simulations are performed over heterogeneous fleets under various temporal tasks and environmental uncertainties.

</details>


### [95] [Fast-SegSim: Real-Time Open-Vocabulary Segmentation for Robotics in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10951)
*Xuan Yu,Yuxuan Xie,Shichao Zhai,Shuhao Ye,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fast-SegSim：基于2D高斯泼溅的实时开放词汇全景重建框架，通过优化渲染流水线实现超过40FPS的渲染速度，用于机器人仿真和感知任务微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建方法（如NeRF或高斯泼溅变体）难以满足机器人控制回路所需的实时推理频率，特别是在处理高维特征进行开放词汇分割时存在过高延迟。

Method: 基于2D高斯泼溅构建端到端框架，核心贡献是高度优化的渲染流水线，包含两项关键优化：精确瓦片相交以减少光栅化冗余，以及新颖的Top-K硬选择策略，利用2D高斯表示的几何稀疏性简化特征累积并缓解带宽限制。

Result: 实现超过40FPS的渲染速率，显著提升实时性能；生成的3D一致输出可作为多视角"真值"标签，用于微调下游感知任务（如目标导航），成功将导航成功率提高一倍。

Conclusion: Fast-SegSim通过优化的渲染流水线实现了实时、高保真、3D一致的开放词汇分割重建，在机器人应用中具有重要价值，既能作为仿真平台的高频传感器输入，又能为下游感知任务提供真值标签，有助于弥合仿真与现实的差距。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary panoptic reconstruction is crucial for advanced robotics and simulation. However, existing 3D reconstruction methods, such as NeRF or Gaussian Splatting variants, often struggle to achieve the real-time inference frequency required by robotic control loops. Existing methods incur prohibitive latency when processing the high-dimensional features required for robust open-vocabulary segmentation. We propose Fast-SegSim, a novel, simple, and end-to-end framework built upon 2D Gaussian Splatting, designed to realize real-time, high-fidelity, and 3D-consistent open-vocabulary segmentation reconstruction. Our core contribution is a highly optimized rendering pipeline that specifically addresses the computational bottleneck of high-channel segmentation feature accumulation. We introduce two key optimizations: Precise Tile Intersection to reduce rasterization redundancy, and a novel Top-K Hard Selection strategy. This strategy leverages the geometric sparsity inherent in the 2D Gaussian representation to greatly simplify feature accumulation and alleviate bandwidth limitations, achieving render rates exceeding 40 FPS. Fast-SegSim provides critical value in robotic applications: it serves both as a high-frequency sensor input for simulation platforms like Gazebo, and its 3D-consistent outputs provide essential multi-view 'ground truth' labels for fine-tuning downstream perception tasks. We demonstrate this utility by using the generated labels to fine-tune the perception module in object goal navigation, successfully doubling the navigation success rate. Our superior rendering speed and practical utility underscore Fast-SegSim's potential to bridge the sim-to-real gap.

</details>


### [96] [Ψ-Map: Panoptic Surface Integrated Mapping Enables Real2Sim Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10982)
*Xuan Yu,Yuxuan Xie,Changjian Jiang,Shichao Zhai,Rong Xiong,Yu Zhang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于大规模场景的开放词汇全景重建框架，结合几何增强、端到端全景学习和高效渲染，在保持40+FPS实时推理的同时实现高质量几何和全景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯泼溅的方法在大规模场景中难以同时实现几何精度、连贯全景理解和实时推理频率，需要综合解决方案。

Method: 1) 利用LiDAR数据构建平面约束多模态高斯混合模型，使用2D高斯面元作为地图表示；2) 设计查询引导的端到端学习架构，通过视锥体内局部交叉注意力将2D掩码特征直接提升到3D空间；3) 引入精确瓦片相交和Top-K硬选择策略优化渲染流水线。

Result: 系统在大规模场景中实现了优越的几何和全景重建质量，同时保持超过40FPS的推理速率，满足机器人控制回路的实时要求。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了大规模开放词汇全景重建中几何精度、全景一致性和实时性能的平衡问题，为机器人感知和仿真提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary panoptic reconstruction is essential for advanced robotics perception and simulation. However, existing methods based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) often struggle to simultaneously achieve geometric accuracy, coherent panoptic understanding, and real-time inference frequency in large-scale scenes. In this paper, we propose a comprehensive framework that integrates geometric reinforcement, end-to-end panoptic learning, and efficient rendering. First, to ensure physical realism in large-scale environments, we leverage LiDAR data to construct plane-constrained multimodal Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) and employ 2D Gaussian surfels as the map representation, enabling high-precision surface alignment and continuous geometric supervision. Building upon this, to overcome the error accumulation and cumbersome cross-frame association inherent in traditional multi-stage panoptic segmentation pipelines, we design a query-guided end-to-end learning architecture. By utilizing a local cross-attention mechanism within the view frustum, the system lifts 2D mask features directly into 3D space, achieving globally consistent panoptic understanding. Finally, addressing the computational bottlenecks caused by high-dimensional semantic features, we introduce Precise Tile Intersection and a Top-K Hard Selection strategy to optimize the rendering pipeline. Experimental results demonstrate that our system achieves superior geometric and panoptic reconstruction quality in large-scale scenes while maintaining an inference rate exceeding 40 FPS, meeting the real-time requirements of robotic control loops.

</details>


### [97] [Inferring World Belief States in Dynamic Real-World Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11020)
*Jack Kolb,Aditya Garg,Nikolai Warner,Karen M. Feigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了在动态3D部分可观测环境中，通过机器人观测来估计人类队友世界信念状态的方法，基于心智模型理论，并应用于人机团队的家庭环境导航任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于心智模型理论，人类决策、情境推理、态势感知和行为规划都依赖于内部模拟或世界信念状态。在团队合作中，心智模型还包括对队友信念和能力的团队模型，从而实现无需持续显式沟通的流畅协作。本研究旨在复制团队模型的核心组件，通过推断人类队友的信念状态（一级态势感知）来提升人机团队在家庭环境中的导航协作。

Method: 研究采用心智模型理论框架，通过机器人在动态、3D、部分可观测环境中的观测来推断人类队友的信念状态（一级态势感知）。方法在真实仿真环境中进行评估，并扩展到真实机器人平台。此外，通过主动辅助语义推理任务展示了信念状态的下游应用。

Result: 在真实仿真环境中成功评估了信念状态推断方法，并将其扩展到真实机器人平台。通过主动辅助语义推理任务，证明了推断出的信念状态在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了在动态3D部分可观测环境中通过机器人观测推断人类队友信念状态的方法，验证了心智模型理论在人机协作中的应用价值，并为实现无需持续显式沟通的流畅人机团队协作提供了技术基础。

Abstract: We investigate estimating a human's world belief state using a robot's observations in a dynamic, 3D, and partially observable environment. The methods are grounded in mental model theory, which posits that human decision making, contextual reasoning, situation awareness, and behavior planning draw from an internal simulation or world belief state. When in teams, the mental model also includes a team model of each teammate's beliefs and capabilities, enabling fluent teamwork without the need for constant and explicit communication. In this work we replicate a core component of the team model by inferring a teammate's belief state, or level one situation awareness, as a human-robot team navigates a household environment. We evaluate our methods in a realistic simulation, extend to a real-world robot platform, and demonstrate a downstream application of the belief state through an active assistance semantic reasoning task.

</details>


### [98] [Federated Single-Agent Robotics: Multi-Robot Coordination Without Intra-Robot Multi-Agent Fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11028)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FSAR提出联邦单智能体机器人架构，反对在单个机器人内部进行多智能体分解，主张通过机器人间的联邦实现多机器人协调，保持每个机器人为单一智能体，在治理局部性和恢复能力方面优于分解密集型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人向规模化集群发展，多机器人协调成为系统挑战。现有方法通常在单个机器人内部进行多智能体分解，但作者认为多机器人协调不需要机器人内部的多智能体碎片化，应保持每个机器人为单一智能体，通过联邦实现协调。

Method: 提出联邦单智能体机器人（FSAR）架构，每个机器人保持单一智能体运行时，具有持久运行时、本地策略范围、能力状态和恢复权限。通过共享能力注册表、跨机器人任务委托、策略感知权限分配、信任范围交互和分层恢复协议实现协调。形式化关键协调关系，包括权限委托、跨机器人能力请求、本地与集群恢复边界、分层人类监督。

Result: 在代表性多机器人协调场景中与分解密集型基线对比，结果显示治理局部性（d=2.91, p<.001 vs. 集中控制）和恢复能力（d=4.88, p<.001 vs. 分解密集型）有统计显著提升，所有场景中权限冲突和策略违规减少。

Conclusion: 从具身智能体到具身集群的路径更适合通过连贯机器人运行时之间的联邦实现，而不是通过机器人内部碎片化。FSAR架构支持共享具身能力模块发现、契约感知的跨机器人协调和集群级治理。

Abstract: As embodied robots move toward fleet-scale operation, multi-robot coordination is becoming a central systems challenge. Existing approaches often treat this as motivation for increasing internal multi-agent decomposition within each robot. We argue for a different principle: multi-robot coordination does not require intra-robot multi-agent fragmentation. Each robot should remain a single embodied agent with its own persistent runtime, local policy scope, capability state, and recovery authority, while coordination emerges through federation across robots at the fleet level. We present Federated Single-Agent Robotics (FSAR), a runtime architecture for multi-robot coordination built on single-agent robot runtimes. Each robot exposes a governed capability surface rather than an internally fragmented agent society. Fleet coordination is achieved through shared capability registries, cross-robot task delegation, policy-aware authority assignment, trust-scoped interaction, and layered recovery protocols. We formalize key coordination relations including authority delegation, inter-robot capability requests, local-versus-fleet recovery boundaries, and hierarchical human supervision, and describe a fleet runtime architecture supporting shared Embodied Capability Module (ECM) discovery, contract-aware cross-robot coordination, and fleet-level governance. We evaluate FSAR on representative multi-robot coordination scenarios against decomposition-heavy baselines. Results show statistically significant gains in governance locality (d=2.91, p<.001 vs. centralized control) and recovery containment (d=4.88, p<.001 vs. decomposition-heavy), while reducing authority conflicts and policy violations across all scenarios. Our results support the view that the path from embodied agents to embodied fleets is better served by federation across coherent robot runtimes than by fragmentation within them.

</details>


### [99] [Simulator Adaptation for Sim-to-Real Learning of Legged Locomotion via Proprioceptive Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11090)
*Jeremy Dao,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于本体感觉分布匹配的模拟器自适应方法，通过比较硬件和模拟器中的关节观测与动作分布来量化差异，无需时间对齐或外部传感，显著提升四足机器人学习策略的仿真到现实迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练的四足运动策略在真实硬件上常因动力学差异导致性能下降，现有方法需要精确的时间对齐匹配和外部传感（如动作捕捉），过程复杂且需要严格控制初始条件，需要更实用的替代方案。

Method: 提出本体感觉分布匹配方法，将硬件和仿真回放视为关节观测和动作的分布进行比较，无需时间对齐或外部传感。将此度量作为黑盒目标，通过参数识别、动作增量模型和残差执行器模型三种方式自适应调整模拟器动力学。

Result: 在Go2四足机器人上进行的仿真到仿真消融实验中，该方法在参数恢复和策略性能提升方面与基于特权状态匹配的基线方法相当。真实世界实验表明，使用不到5分钟的硬件数据即可显著减少漂移，即使在具有挑战性的两足行走行为中也表现良好。

Conclusion: 本体感觉分布匹配为学习型四足运动的仿真到现实迁移提供了一条实用有效的模拟器自适应路径，简化了硬件数据收集要求，提升了策略在真实世界中的性能表现。

Abstract: Simulation trained legged locomotion policies often exhibit performance loss on hardware due to dynamics discrepancies between the simulator and the real world, highlighting the need for approaches that adapt the simulator itself to better match hardware behavior. Prior work typically quantify these discrepancies through precise, time-aligned matching of joint and base trajectories. This process requires motion capture, privileged sensing, and carefully controlled initial conditions. We introduce a practical alternative based on proprioceptive distribution matching, which compares hardware and simulation rollouts as distributions of joint observations and actions, eliminating the need for time alignment or external sensing. Using this metric as a black-box objective, we explore adapting simulator dynamics through parameter identification, action-delta models, and residual actuator models. Our approach matches the parameter recovery and policy-performance gains of privileged state-matching baselines across extensive sim-to-sim ablations on the Go2 quadruped. Real-world experiments demonstrate substantial drift reduction using less than five minutes of hardware data, even for a challenging two-legged walking behavior. These results demonstrate that proprioceptive distribution matching provides a practical and effective route to simulator adaptation for sim-to-real transfer of learned legged locomotion.

</details>


### [100] [AIM: Intent-Aware Unified world action Modeling with Spatial Value Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11135)
*Liaoyuan Fan,Zetian Xu,Chen Cao,Wenyao Zhang,Mingqi Yuan,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AIM是一个意图感知的统一世界动作模型，通过显式的空间接口桥接视频生成模型与机器人控制，使用空间价值图编码任务相关交互结构，并通过自蒸馏强化学习优化动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一世界动作模型直接从未来视觉表示解码动作，缺乏对交互位置和操纵意图的显式推理，导致动作不可靠。视频生成模型捕捉场景演化，但动作生成需要空间意图建模。

Method: 1) 预测对齐的空间价值图编码任务相关交互结构；2) 基于预训练视频生成模型，在共享混合变换器架构中联合建模未来观测和价值图；3) 使用意图因果注意力将未来信息通过价值表示路由到动作分支；4) 自蒸馏强化学习阶段冻结视频和价值分支，仅优化动作头。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0基准测试中达到94.0%的平均成功率，显著优于先前统一世界动作基线。在长时域和接触敏感操纵任务中改进尤为明显。

Conclusion: 显式空间意图建模作为视觉世界建模与机器人控制之间的桥梁是有效的。空间价值图提供了控制导向的未来动态抽象，意图因果注意力和自蒸馏强化学习实现了可靠的动作生成。

Abstract: Pretrained video generation models provide strong priors for robot control, but existing unified world action models still struggle to decode reliable actions without substantial robot-specific training. We attribute this limitation to a structural mismatch: while video models capture how scenes evolve, action generation requires explicit reasoning about where to interact and the underlying manipulation intent. We introduce AIM, an intent-aware unified world action model that bridges this gap via an explicit spatial interface. Instead of decoding actions directly from future visual representations, AIM predicts an aligned spatial value map that encodes task-relevant interaction structure, enabling a control-oriented abstraction of future dynamics. Built on a pretrained video generation model, AIM jointly models future observations and value maps within a shared mixture-of-transformers architecture. It employs intent-causal attention to route future information to the action branch exclusively through the value representation. We further propose a self-distillation reinforcement learning stage that freezes the video and value branches and optimizes only the action head using dense rewards derived from projected value-map responses together with sparse task-level signals. To support training and evaluation, we construct a simulation dataset of 30K manipulation trajectories with synchronized multi-view observations, actions, and value-map annotations. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark show that AIM achieves a 94.0% average success rate, significantly outperforming prior unified world action baselines. Notably, the improvement is more pronounced in long-horizon and contact-sensitive manipulation tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of explicit spatial-intent modeling as a bridge between visual world modeling and robot control.

</details>


### [101] [EmbodiedGovBench: A Benchmark for Governance, Recovery, and Upgrade Safety in Embodied Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11174)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiedGovBench：面向具身智能系统治理评估的基准，关注可控性、策略边界、可恢复性等治理维度，而非仅任务成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身AI评估主要关注任务成功率（如完成率、操作精度），但缺乏对系统治理能力的评估，包括是否尊重能力边界、执行策略、安全恢复、维护审计跟踪和响应人类监督

Method: 提出EmbodiedGovBench基准框架，涵盖七个治理维度：未授权能力调用、运行时漂移鲁棒性、恢复成功率、策略可移植性、版本升级安全性、人类覆盖响应性、审计完整性。包含单机器人和集群设置、场景模板、扰动算子、治理指标和基线评估协议

Result: 建立了首个面向具身智能系统治理的评估框架，定义了完整的基准结构和评估维度，可实例化到具有模块化接口和合约感知升级工作流的具身能力运行时中

Conclusion: 具身治理应成为一等评估目标，EmbodiedGovBench为这一转变提供了初始测量框架，填补了当前评估中治理能力衡量的关键空白

Abstract: Recent progress in embodied AI has produced a growing ecosystem of robot policies, foundation models, and modular runtimes. However, current evaluation remains dominated by task success metrics such as completion rate or manipulation accuracy. These metrics leave a critical gap: they do not measure whether embodied systems are governable -- whether they respect capability boundaries, enforce policies, recover safely, maintain audit trails, and respond to human oversight. We present EmbodiedGovBench, a benchmark for governance-oriented evaluation of embodied agent systems. Rather than asking only whether a robot can complete a task, EmbodiedGovBench evaluates whether the system remains controllable, policy-bounded, recoverable, auditable, and evolution-safe under realistic perturbations. The benchmark covers seven governance dimensions: unauthorized capability invocation, runtime drift robustness, recovery success, policy portability, version upgrade safety, human override responsiveness, and audit completeness. We define a benchmark structure spanning single-robot and fleet settings, with scenario templates, perturbation operators, governance metrics, and baseline evaluation protocols. We describe how the benchmark can be instantiated over embodied capability runtimes with modular interfaces and contract-aware upgrade workflows. Our analysis suggests that embodied governance should become a first-class evaluation target. EmbodiedGovBench provides the initial measurement framework for that shift.

</details>


### [102] [CLAW: Composable Language-Annotated Whole-body Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11251)
*Jianuo Cao,Yuxin Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAW是一个基于Web的交互式流水线，用于为Unitree G1人形机器人生成语言标注的全身运动数据，通过组合运动模块和模板化语言标注实现可扩展的数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：基于动作捕捉的方法成本高且多样性有限；文本到运动生成模型产生的是纯运动学输出，不能保证物理可行性。需要一种可扩展的方法来生成语言标注的全身运动数据。

Method: CLAW将运动规划器的运动模式视为可组合的构建模块，每个模块由运动、朝向、速度、骨盆高度和持续时间参数化。提供两种浏览器界面：实时键盘模式和基于时间线的序列编辑器。使用低层全身控制器在MuJoCo仿真中跟踪规划器的运动学参考，同时使用确定性模板化标注引擎为每个片段和完整轨迹生成多样化的自然语言描述。

Result: 开发了一个开源系统，支持以50Hz频率记录物理基础轨迹，并生成多种风格的自然语言描述，为人形机器人学习提供可扩展的语言-运动配对数据生成能力。

Conclusion: CLAW提供了一个可扩展的解决方案，用于生成语言标注的全身运动数据，解决了现有方法的局限性，并通过开源发布支持人形机器人学习社区的发展。

Abstract: Training language-conditioned whole-body controllers for humanoid robots requires large-scale datasets pairing motion trajectories with natural-language descriptions.Existing approaches based on motion capture are costly and limited in diversity, while text-to-motion generative models produce purely kinematic outputs that are not guaranteed to be physically feasible.Therefore, we present CLAW, an interactive web-based pipeline for scalable generation of language-annotated whole-body motion data for the Unitree G1 humanoid robot. CLAW treats the motion modes of a kinematic planner as composable building blocks, each parameterized by movement, heading, speed, pelvis height and duration, and provides two browser-based interfaces -- a real-time keyboard mode and a timeline-based sequence editor -- for exploratory and batch data collection. A low-level whole-body controller tracks the planner's kinematic references in MuJoCo simulation, producing physically grounded trajectories recorded at 50Hz. Simultaneously, a deterministic template-based annotation engine generates diverse natural-language descriptions at multiple stylistic registers for every segment and for the full trajectory. We release the system as open source to support scalable generation of language-motion paired data for humanoid robot learning.

</details>


### [103] [Modeling, Analysis and Activation of Planar Viscoelastically-combined Rimless Wheels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11295)
*Fumihiko Asano,Yuxuan Xiang,Yanqiu Zheng,Cong Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两种新型被动动态步行器VCRW1和VCRW2，通过粘弹性元件连接两个十字形框架，分析其步态特性并探讨作为步行辅助设备的潜力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发新型被动动态步行器，通过粘弹性元件组合两个四腿无轮缘轮，探索其作为步行辅助设备的可能性

Method: 提出两种VCRW模型：VCRW1（希腊十字形框架）和VCRW2（可自由旋转的两连杆十字形框架）；建立运动方程和碰撞模型；进行数值分析研究被动动态行走特性；对VCRW2进行激活以生成稳定水平步态

Result: 建立了VCRW的数学模型，分析了两种设计的被动动态行走基本步态特性，实现了VCRW2的稳定水平步态生成

Conclusion: VCRW作为一种新型被动动态步行器具有作为步行辅助设备的潜力，特别是VCRW2通过激活可实现稳定水平行走

Abstract: This paper proposes novel passive-dynamic walkers formed by two cross-shaped frames and eight viscoelastic elements. Since it is a combination of two four-legged rimless wheels via viscoelastic elements, we call it viscoelastically-combined rimless wheel (VCRW). Two types of VCRWs consisting of different cross-shaped frames are introduced; one is formed by combining two Greek-cross-shaped frames (VCRW1), and the other is formed by combining two-link cross-shaped frames that can rotate freely around the central axis (VCRW2). First, we describe the model assumptions and equations of motion and collision. Second, we numerically analyze the basic gait properties of passive dynamic walking. Furthermore, we consider an activation of VCRW2 for generating a stable level gait, and discuss the significance of the study as a novel walking support device.

</details>


### [104] [Learning to Forget -- Hierarchical Episodic Memory for Lifelong Robot Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11306)
*Leonard Bärmann,Joana Plewnia,Alex Waibel,Tamim Asfour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H²-EMV框架让机器人通过用户交互学习选择性记忆，构建分层情景记忆，基于语言模型估计相关性进行选择性遗忘，并通过用户反馈更新遗忘规则，实现可扩展的个性化记忆管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要回答关于过去经历的问题，但持续的多模态感知会产生海量情景记忆，超出存储限制且查询效率低下，需要根据用户相关性的概念进行选择性遗忘。

Method: 提出H²-EMV框架：增量构建分层情景记忆；使用基于语言模型的相关性估计进行选择性遗忘，条件于学习的自然语言规则；根据用户对遗忘细节的反馈更新这些规则。

Result: 在模拟家务任务和20.5小时真实世界记录上的评估显示：H²-EMV在保持问答准确性的同时，减少45%内存大小和35%查询时间计算；第二轮查询准确率提高70%，表明能适应用户特定优先级。

Conclusion: 学习性遗忘使机器人能够实现可扩展、个性化的情景记忆，支持长期人机协作，通过适应用户优先级随时间改进性能。

Abstract: Robots must verbalize their past experiences when users ask "Where did you put my keys?" or "Why did the task fail?" Yet maintaining life-long episodic memory (EM) from continuous multimodal perception quickly exceeds storage limits and makes real-time query impractical, calling for selective forgetting that adapts to users' notions of relevance. We present H$^2$-EMV, a framework enabling humanoids to learn what to remember through user interaction. Our approach incrementally constructs hierarchical EM, selectively forgets using language-model-based relevance estimation conditioned on learned natural-language rules, and updates these rules given user feedback about forgotten details. Evaluations on simulated household tasks and 20.5-hour-long real-world recordings from ARMAR-7 demonstrate that H$^2$-EMV maintains question-answering accuracy while reducing memory size by 45% and query-time compute by 35%. Critically, performance improves over time - accuracy increases 70% in second-round queries by adapting to user-specific priorities - demonstrating that learned forgetting enables scalable, personalized EM for long-term human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [105] [CLASP: Closed-loop Asynchronous Spatial Perception for Open-vocabulary Desktop Object Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11320)
*Yiran Ling,Wenxuan Li,Siying Dong,Yize Zhang,Xiaoyao Huang,Jing Jiang,Ruonan Li,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLASP是一个用于机器人桌面抓取的异步闭环框架，通过双路径分层感知、异步闭环评估器和可扩展多模态数据引擎，解决了VLM在抓取任务中的空间幻觉、开环执行脆弱性和高质量数据稀缺问题，实现了87.0%的整体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在机器人操作中显示出强大潜力，但在低级抓取任务部署中面临三个关键挑战：高质量多模态演示数据稀缺、弱几何基础导致的空间幻觉，以及动态环境中开环执行的脆弱性。这些限制阻碍了VLM在实际机器人抓取任务中的有效应用。

Method: 1. 双路径分层感知模块：将高层语义意图与几何基础解耦，引导推理模型输出和确定动作元组，减少空间幻觉。
2. 异步闭环评估器：比较执行前后的状态，提供基于文本的诊断反馈，建立稳健的错误纠正循环。
3. 可扩展多模态数据引擎：从真实和合成场景自动合成高质量空间标注和推理模板，无需人工遥操作。

Result: 该方法显著优于现有基线，达到87.0%的整体成功率。框架表现出卓越的泛化能力，能够跨越不同物体，弥合仿真到现实的差距，在几何挑战性类别和杂乱场景中提供出色的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CLASP框架通过整合多模态感知、逻辑推理和状态反射反馈，有效解决了VLM在机器人抓取中的关键挑战。异步闭环设计显著提升了动态环境中的执行鲁棒性，而自动化的数据生成方法为高质量训练数据提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Robot grasping of desktop object is widely used in intelligent manufacturing, logistics, and agriculture.Although vision-language models (VLMs) show strong potential for robotic manipulation, their deployment in low-level grasping faces key challenges: scarce high-quality multimodal demonstrations, spatial hallucination caused by weak geometric grounding, and the fragility of open-loop execution in dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we propose Closed-Loop Asynchronous Spatial Perception(CLASP), a novel asynchronous closed-loop framework that integrates multimodal perception, logical reasoning, and state-reflective feedback. First, we design a Dual-Pathway Hierarchical Perception module that decouples high-level semantic intent from geometric grounding. The design guides the output of the inference model and the definite action tuples, reducing spatial illusions. Second, an Asynchronous Closed-Loop Evaluator is implemented to compare pre- and post-execution states, providing text-based diagnostic feedback to establish a robust error-correction loop and improving the vulnerability of traditional open-loop execution in dynamic environments. Finally, we design a scalable multi-modal data engine that automatically synthesizes high-quality spatial annotations and reasoning templates from real and synthetic scenes without human teleoperation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving an 87.0% overall success rate. Notably, the proposed framework exhibits remarkable generalization across diverse objects, bridging the sim-to-real gap and providing exceptional robustness in geometrically challenging categories and cluttered scenarios.

</details>


### [106] [Learning Racket-Ball Bounce Dynamics Across Diverse Rubbers for Robotic Table Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11349)
*Thomas Gossard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于高斯过程的球拍-乒乓球碰撞统一建模框架，覆盖10种不同胶皮类型，显著提升反弹速度和旋转预测精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有乒乓球机器人控制中的球拍-球碰撞模型通常假设简单线性模型且仅限于反胶，无法泛化到实践中遇到的各种球拍类型，限制了机器人系统的适应性和可靠性。

Method: 使用高速多摄像头系统采集10种不同胶皮类型（反胶、防弧胶、颗粒胶）的球拍-球碰撞数据集；采用基于冲量的接触模型，使用高斯过程回归估计恢复系数和切向冲量响应等关键物理参数，这些参数被建模为入射速度和旋转的函数。

Result: 相比恒定参数基线模型，该方法在所有球拍类型上都降低了反弹后速度和旋转的预测误差，对非标准胶皮的改进最为显著；高斯过程模型还能通过少量观测实现在线识别球拍动力学特性。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架能够准确建模不同胶皮类型的球拍-球碰撞动力学，提供物理可解释性和不确定性估计，为机器人乒乓球系统提供了更可靠的控制基础。

Abstract: Accurate dynamic models for racket-ball bounces are essential for reliable control in robotic table tennis. Existing models typically assume simple linear models and are restricted to inverted rubbers, limiting their ability to generalize across the wide variety of rackets encountered in practice. In this work, we present a unified framework for modeling ball-racket interactions across 10 racket configurations featuring different rubber types, including inverted, anti-spin, and pimpled surfaces. Using a high-speed multi-camera setup with spin estimation, we collect a dataset of racket-ball bounces spanning a broad range of incident velocities and spins. We show that key physical parameters governing rebound, such as the Coefficient of Restitution and tangential impulse response, vary systematically with the impact state and differ significantly across rubbers. To capture these effects while preserving physical interpretability, we estimate the parameters of an impulse-based contact model using Gaussian Processes conditioned on the ball's incoming velocity and spin. The resulting model provides both accurate predictions and uncertainty estimations. Compared to the constant parameter baselines, our approach reduces post-impact velocity and spin prediction errors across all racket types, with the largest improvements observed for nonstandard rubbers. Furthermore, the GP-based model enables online identification of racket dynamics with few observations during gameplay.

</details>


### [107] [WM-DAgger: Enabling Efficient Data Aggregation for Imitation Learning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11351)
*Anlan Yu,Zaishu Chen,Peili Song,Zhiqing Hong,Haotian Wang,Desheng Zhang,Tian He,Yi Ding,Daqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WM-DAgger：利用世界模型合成OOD恢复数据的高效数据聚合框架，无需人类参与，显著提高模仿学习在机器人操作任务中的成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习存在复合误差问题：微小的策略不准确会导致机器人进入训练集中未见过的分布外状态，进而产生更大错误并最终失败。虽然DAgger框架试图解决此问题，但其依赖持续人类参与严重限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出WM-DAgger框架，利用世界模型合成OOD恢复数据而无需人类参与。针对眼手机器人臂的操纵任务和少量演示，引入两个关键机制：(1) 纠正动作合成模块生成面向任务的恢复动作以防止误导监督；(2) 一致性引导过滤模块通过将终端合成帧锚定到专家演示中的对应真实帧来丢弃物理上不可行的轨迹。

Result: 在多个真实世界机器人任务上广泛验证，方法显著提高了成功率，在软袋推动任务中仅用五个演示就达到了93.3%的成功率。

Conclusion: WM-DAgger通过世界模型合成OOD恢复数据，有效解决了模仿学习的复合误差问题，同时避免了DAgger对人类参与的依赖，实现了高效可扩展的数据聚合。

Abstract: Imitation learning is a powerful paradigm for training robotic policies, yet its performance is limited by compounding errors: minor policy inaccuracies could drive robots into unseen out-of-distribution (OOD) states in the training set, where the policy could generate even bigger errors, leading to eventual failures. While the Data Aggregation (DAgger) framework tries to address this issue, its reliance on continuous human involvement severely limits scalability. In this paper, we propose WM-DAgger, an efficient data aggregation framework that leverages World Models to synthesize OOD recovery data without requiring human involvement. Specifically, we focus on manipulation tasks with an eye-in-hand robotic arm and only few-shot demonstrations. To avoid synthesizing misleading data and overcome the hallucination issues inherent to World Models, our framework introduces two key mechanisms: (1) a Corrective Action Synthesis Module that generates task-oriented recovery actions to prevent misleading supervision, and (2) a Consistency-Guided Filtering Module that discards physically implausible trajectories by anchoring terminal synthesized frames to corresponding real frames in expert demonstrations. We extensively validate WM-DAgger on multiple real-world robotic tasks. Results that our method significantly improves success rates, achieving a 93.3\% success rate in soft bag pushing with only five demonstrations. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/czs12354-xxdbd/WM-Dagger.

</details>


### [108] [MR.ScaleMaster: Scale-Consistent Collaborative Mapping from Crowd-Sourced Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11372)
*Hyoseok Ju,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MR.ScaleMaster是一个用于众包单目视频的协同建图系统，通过Scale Collapse Alarm机制拒绝虚假闭环、Sim(3)锚节点解决尺度模糊性、模块化接口实现即插即用，显著提升多机器人单目SLAM的尺度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包单目相机协同建图可实现无需专用传感器的可扩展3D重建，但面临两个尺度相关的失效模式：重复环境中虚假闭环导致的尺度崩溃，以及长轨迹中的渐进尺度漂移和每机器人尺度模糊性阻碍多会话融合。

Method: 提出MR.ScaleMaster系统，包含三个关键机制：1) Scale Collapse Alarm在虚假闭环破坏位姿图前拒绝它们；2) Sim(3)锚节点公式将经典SE(3)框架推广到显式估计每会话尺度，解决每机器人尺度模糊性并强制全局尺度一致性；3) 模块化开源即插即用接口，允许任何单目重建模型无需后端修改即可集成。

Result: 在最多15个智能体的KITTI序列上，Sim(3)公式相比SE(3)基线实现了7.2倍的ATE减少，警报机制拒绝了所有虚假闭环同时保留了每个有效约束。进一步演示了在单个统一地图中融合MASt3R-SLAM、pi3和VGGT-SLAM 2.0的异构多机器人稠密建图。

Conclusion: MR.ScaleMaster通过创新的尺度崩溃警报、Sim(3)锚节点公式和模块化接口，有效解决了众包单目协同建图中的尺度崩溃和尺度漂移问题，实现了鲁棒的多机器人尺度一致性建图。

Abstract: Crowd-sourced cooperative mapping from monocular cameras promises scalable 3D reconstruction without specialized sensors, yet remains hindered by two scale-specific failure modes: abrupt scale collapse from false-positive loop closures in repetitive environments, and gradual scale drift over long trajectories and per-robot scale ambiguity that prevent direct multi-session fusion. We present MR.ScaleMaster, a cooperative mapping system for crowd-sourced monocular videos that addresses both failure modes. MR.ScaleMaster introduces three key mechanisms. First, a Scale Collapse Alarm rejects spurious loop closures before they corrupt the pose graph. Second, a Sim(3) anchor node formulation generalizes the classical SE(3) framework to explicitly estimate per-session scale, resolving per-robot scale ambiguity and enforcing global scale consistency. Third, a modular, open-source, plug-and-play interface enables any monocular reconstruction model to integrate without backend modification. On KITTI sequences with up to 15 agents, the Sim(3) formulation achieves a 7.2x ATE reduction over the SE(3) baseline, and the alarm rejects all false-positive loops while preserving every valid constraint. We further demonstrate heterogeneous multi-robot dense mapping fusing MASt3R-SLAM, pi3, and VGGT-SLAM 2.0 within a single unified map.

</details>


### [109] [ComSim: Building Scalable Real-World Robot Data Generation via Compositional Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11386)
*Yiran Qin,Jiahua Ma,Li Kang,Wenzhan Li,Yihang Jiao,Xin Wen,Xiufeng Song,Heng Zhou,Jiwen Yu,Zhenfei Yin,Xihui Liu,Philip Torr,Yilun Du,Ruimao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Compositional Simulation方法，结合经典仿真与神经仿真生成动作-视频对，通过闭环真实-仿真-真实数据增强管道，利用少量真实数据生成大规模多样化训练数据集，显著减小sim2real领域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域基础模型（如大语言模型和世界模型）的发展提升了机器人自主执行复杂任务的能力，但获取大规模高质量训练数据仍面临挑战：需要大量人工努力且难以覆盖多样化的真实世界环境。

Method: 提出Compositional Simulation混合方法，结合经典仿真和神经仿真生成准确的动作-视频对并保持真实世界一致性。采用闭环真实-仿真-真实数据增强管道，利用少量真实数据生成大规模多样化训练数据集。训练神经仿真器将经典仿真视频转换为真实世界表示，提升在真实环境中训练的策略模型的准确性。

Result: 通过大量实验证明，该方法显著减小了sim2real领域差距，在真实世界策略模型训练中获得了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 该方法为生成鲁棒训练数据和弥合仿真与真实世界机器人之间的差距提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in foundational models, such as large language models and world models, have greatly enhanced the capabilities of robotics, enabling robots to autonomously perform complex tasks. However, acquiring large-scale, high-quality training data for robotics remains a challenge, as it often requires substantial manual effort and is limited in its coverage of diverse real-world environments. To address this, we propose a novel hybrid approach called Compositional Simulation, which combines classical simulation and neural simulation to generate accurate action-video pairs while maintaining real-world consistency. Our approach utilizes a closed-loop real-sim-real data augmentation pipeline, leveraging a small amount of real-world data to generate diverse, large-scale training datasets that cover a broader spectrum of real-world scenarios. We train a neural simulator to transform classical simulation videos into real-world representations, improving the accuracy of policy models trained in real-world environments. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our method significantly reduces the sim2real domain gap, resulting in higher success rates in real-world policy model training. Our approach offers a scalable solution for generating robust training data and bridging the gap between simulated and real-world robotics.

</details>


### [110] [EagleVision: A Multi-Task Benchmark for Cross-Domain Perception in High-Speed Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11400)
*Zakhar Yagudin,Murad Mebrahtu,Ren Jin,Jiaqi Huang,Yujia Yue,Dzmitry Tsetserukou,Jorge Dias,Majid Khonji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EagleVision是一个用于高速自动驾驶赛车的统一LiDAR多任务基准，包含真实赛车数据和模拟数据，用于系统研究极端高速动态下的感知泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速自主赛车面临极端感知挑战，包括大相对速度和与传统城市驾驶数据集的显著领域偏移。现有基准无法充分捕捉这些高动态条件，需要专门针对高速赛车场景的基准来研究感知泛化能力。

Method: 引入EagleVision基准，整合了Indy Autonomous Challenge数据集（14,893帧）、A2RL Real竞赛数据集（1,163帧）和12,000帧模拟生成标注帧，所有数据采用统一的评估协议。使用以数据集为中心的迁移框架量化城市、模拟和真实赛车领域之间的跨领域泛化能力。

Result: 城市预训练相比从头训练提高了检测性能（NDS 0.72 vs. 0.69），而在真实赛车数据上进行中间预训练实现了到A2RL的最佳迁移（NDS 0.726），优于仅使用模拟器的适应。对于轨迹预测，Indy训练的模型在A2RL测试序列上优于域内A2RL训练（FDE 0.947 vs. 1.250），突显了运动分布覆盖在跨领域预测中的重要性。

Conclusion: EagleVision基准能够系统研究极端高速动态下的感知泛化，展示了领域迁移策略对高速赛车感知任务的重要性，为高速自动驾驶赛车的研究提供了标准化评估框架和公开数据集。

Abstract: High-speed autonomous racing presents extreme perception challenges, including large relative velocities and substantial domain shifts from conventional urban-driving datasets. Existing benchmarks do not adequately capture these high-dynamic conditions. We introduce EagleVision, a unified LiDAR-based multi-task benchmark for 3D detection and trajectory prediction in high-speed racing, providing newly annotated 3D bounding boxes for the Indy Autonomous Challenge dataset (14,893 frames) and the A2RL Real competition dataset (1,163 frames), together with 12,000 simulator-generated annotated frames, all standardized under a common evaluation protocol. Using a dataset-centric transfer framework, we quantify cross-domain generalization across urban, simulator, and real racing domains. Urban pretraining improves detection over scratch training (NDS 0.72 vs. 0.69), while intermediate pretraining on real racing data achieves the best transfer to A2RL (NDS 0.726), outperforming simulator-only adaptation. For trajectory prediction, Indy-trained models surpass in-domain A2RL training on A2RL test sequences (FDE 0.947 vs. 1.250), highlighting the role of motion-distribution coverage in cross-domain forecasting. EagleVision enables systematic study of perception generalization under extreme high-speed dynamics. The dataset and benchmark are publicly available at https://avlab.io/EagleVision

</details>


### [111] [Using Unwrapped Full Color Space Palette Recording to Measure Exposedness of a Vehicle Exterior Parts for External Human Machine Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11406)
*Jaerock Kwon,Jose Gonzalez-Belmonte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过3D模拟分析，确定了在四路交叉口行人视野中2015款福特F-150 King Ranch车辆各部位的可见频率，为eHMI（外部人机界面）的优化布局提供数据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆需要向行人等道路使用者有效传达其意图以防止事故。外部人机界面(eHMIs)是解决这一问题的方案，但需要确定在车辆外部的最佳安装位置以确保可见性。

Method: 采用网格面展开到纹理的技术（每个像素分配唯一颜色），在Unity游戏引擎中创建并运行一系列动画模拟，测量在四路交叉口行人过马路时，车辆各部位被遮挡的频率。

Result: 研究发现保险杠、格栅和引擎盖是行人最常看到的部位，但同车道其他车辆可能遮挡这些区域，使其作为eHMI安装位置不够可靠。因此建议采用分布式方法，同时使用挡风玻璃和前翼子板作为eHMI安装位置。

Conclusion: 为确保持续可见性，eHMI应采用分布式布局策略，结合挡风玻璃和前翼子板等多个位置，避免因其他车辆遮挡导致通信失效。

Abstract: One of the concerns with autonomous vehicles is their ability to communicate their intent to other road users, specially pedestrians, in order to prevent accidents. External Human-Machine Interfaces (eHMIs) are the proposed solution to this issue, through the introduction of electronic devices on the exterior of a vehicle that communicate when the vehicle is planning on slowing down or yielding. This paper uses the technique of unwrapping the faces of a mesh onto a texture where every pixel is a unique color, as well as a series of animated simulations made and ran in the Unity game engine, to measure how many times is each point on a 2015 Ford F-150 King Ranch is unobstructed to a pedestrian attempting to cross the road at a four-way intersection. By cross-referencing the results with a color-coded map of the labeled parts on the exterior of the vehicle, it was concluded that while the bumper, grill, and hood were the parts of the vehicle visible to the crossing pedestrian most often, the existence of other vehicles on the same lane that might obstruct the view of these makes them insufficient. The study recommends instead a distributive approach to eHMIs by using both the windshield and frontal fenders as simultaneous placements for these devices.

</details>


### [112] [Dyadic Partnership(DP): A Missing Link Towards Full Autonomy in Medical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11423)
*Nassir Navab,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"二元伙伴关系"新范式，让机器人与临床医生进行智能专家互动协作，通过生成AI和多模态可视化实现决策共识，作为向完全自主过渡的可行里程碑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统医疗机器人主要基于遥操作，虽然设计智能但认知和决策仍依赖外科医生。随着医疗机器人向高级自主性发展，需要探索向部分和完全自主的过渡路径。

Method: 引入二元伙伴关系范式，机器人与临床医生通过动态交互协作，利用生成AI（如世界模型）和多模态可视化进行决策讨论和共识达成。包括临床智能基础模型、多模态意图识别、共同学习框架、高级可视化和可解释信任感知交互等核心组件。

Result: 提出了一种新的协作范式，为向完全自主过渡提供了可行且可接受的里程碑。该框架能够实现更安全、更直观的协作，并逐步过渡到不同临床环境中的完全自主。

Conclusion: 二元伙伴关系是医疗机器人向自主性发展的强大且可实现的里程碑，通过智能协作解决数据稀缺、标准化不足和伦理接受等关键挑战，为更安全直观的临床协作提供有前景的路径。

Abstract: For the past decades medical robotic solutions were mostly based on the concept of tele-manipulation. While their design was extremely intelligent, allowing for better access, improved dexterity, reduced tremor, and improved imaging, their intelligence was limited. They therefore left cognition and decision making to the surgeon. As medical robotics advances towards high-level autonomy, the scientific community needs to explore the required pathway towards partial and full autonomy. Here, we introduce the concept of Dyadic Partnership(DP), a new paradigm in which robots and clinicians engage in intelligent, expert interaction and collaboration. The Dyadic Partners would discuss and agree on decisions and actions during their dynamic and interactive collaboration relying also on intuitive advanced media using generative AI, such as a world model, and advanced multi-modal visualization. This article outlines the foundational components needed to enable such systems, including foundation models for clinical intelligence, multi-modal intent recognition, co-learning frameworks, advanced visualization, and explainable, trust-aware interaction. We further discuss key challenges such as data scarcity, lack of standardization, and ethical acceptance. Dyadic partnership is introduced and is positioned as a powerful yet achievable, acceptable milestone offering a promising pathway toward safer, more intuitive collaboration and a gradual transition to full autonomy across diverse clinical settings.

</details>


### [113] [Safe Human-to-Humanoid Motion Imitation Using Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11447)
*Wenqi Cai,John Abanes,Nikolaos Evangeliou,Anthony Tzes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉的人形机器人运动模仿框架，通过控制屏障函数确保操作安全，避免自碰撞和人机碰撞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人形机器人模仿人类运动时，确保操作安全至关重要，需要避免机器人自碰撞和人机碰撞

Method: 使用单摄像头捕捉人体骨骼关键点并转换为关节角度进行运动重定向；通过基于二次规划的控制屏障函数层过滤模仿指令，防止碰撞

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提框架在实时碰撞感知运动模仿中的有效性

Conclusion: 该视觉框架能够实现安全的人形机器人运动模仿，控制屏障函数方法有效防止碰撞

Abstract: Ensuring operational safety is critical for human-to-humanoid motion imitation. This paper presents a vision-based framework that enables a humanoid robot to imitate human movements while avoiding collisions. Human skeletal keypoints are captured by a single camera and converted into joint angles for motion retargeting.
